# SvH Testgen — Codebase Walkthrough (deep, per-test)

**Document role:** **Phase 3** (codebase audit). Write or refresh **after** `SvH-Testgen-Plan.md` is approved and `SvH*.py` + generated `.S` exist.  
**Phase 1** (plan steps, coverpoint counts): `docs/SvH-Testgen-Plan.md` — reviewer reads that first.

**As-of:** 2026-08-25 (live `SvH*.py` + `.S`; per-test **mapping rules** + **missing deps**).  
**Repo root:** `riscv-arch-test/`  
**Stimulus SoT:** `generators/testgen/src/testgen/priv/extensions/SvH*.py`  
**Generated output:** `tests/priv/SvH/SvH_<stem>-00.S` (**39** files) — **do not hand-edit**.

This document traces **Python → asm string list → `.S` → ELF → Sail/CPU → `.rvvi` → `SvH_cg`**. Every per-test chapter cites live function names and line ranges. Behavior outside `SvH*.py` is labeled **MISSING DEPENDENCY**.

---

## 0. Files in scope

| File | Role |
|------|------|
| `…/extensions/SvH.py` | Entry `@add_priv_test_generator` → `make_svh`; explicit `_SCENARIOS` (39 tuples) |
| `…/extensions/SvHCommon.py` | Shared emitters: twins, VA/GPA `.set`, PTE macros, hops, SIGUPD, `.data` |
| `…/extensions/SvH_twostage.py` | 7 generators (paging on/off, ifetch, MXR) |
| `…/extensions/SvH_csr.py` | 3 generators (hgatp / vsatp / satp fields) |
| `…/extensions/SvH_perm.py` | 13 generators (perms, SUM, MXR×SUM, x-only) |
| `…/extensions/SvH_fault.py` | 3 generators (invalid PTE / faults) |
| `…/extensions/SvH_misc.py` | 13 generators (HFENCE, MPRV, TVM, attrs, VSBE) — local `_finish_twin_test` |
| `tests/priv/SvH/*.S` | Autogenerated assembly |

**Offline only (not opened at runtime):** `…/extensions/_park/svh_seed/`.

---

## 1. End-to-end data flow

```text
make testgen EXTENSIONS=SvH EXCLUDE_EXTENSIONS=Sm
        │
        ▼
testgen loads registered priv generators
        │  MISSING DEPENDENCY: generators/testgen registry + TestData / TestChunk writer
        ▼
SvH.py :: make_svh(test_data)
        │  1) delete_old_asm_files()          → wipe tests/priv/SvH/*.S
        │  2) for (stem, generate_fn) in _SCENARIOS:
        │       begin_test_chunk(stem)
        │       chunk.code.extend(generate_fn(test_data))   # list[str]
        │       end_test_chunk()
        ▼
Framework wraps each chunk:
  header (#define SIGUPD_COUNT, RVTEST_HYPERVISOR, TRAP_SIGUPD_COUNT)
  #include env headers
  RVTEST_BEGIN … body … RVTEST_END / signature / data
        │  MISSING DEPENDENCY: tests/env hypervisor macros (G_PTE_SETUP, hops, SIGUPD)
        ▼
tests/priv/SvH/SvH_<stem>-00.S
        │
        ▼
make elfs / GCC assemble + link
        │  MISSING DEPENDENCY: linker script, crt0, -march from TEST_CONFIG
        ▼
ELF → Sail (--trace) → *.trace → sail_to_rvvi → *.rvvi
        │  MISSING DEPENDENCY: sail model; framework/src/act/sail_to_rvvi.py
        ▼
Questa/VCS TB samples coverpoints/priv/SvH_coverage.svh (SvH_cg)
        │  MISSING DEPENDENCY: verification-licensed simulator (Starter cannot sample)
        ▼
UCDB / uncovered report
```

**Python never executes RISC-V.** It only builds `list[str]`.

**Per-test steps:** see §6 — each T01–T39 chapter ends with plain-English steps (not the old generic “mapping rules” block).

---

## 2. Orchestrator — `SvH.py`

### 2.1 Registration

- Imports every public `generate_*` from the five family modules.
- `@add_priv_test_generator("SvH", required_extensions=["I","H"], …)` registers `make_svh`.
- `extra_defines` inject `#define RVTEST_HYPERVISOR` and `#define TRAP_SIGUPD_COUNT 4096`.

### 2.2 `_SCENARIOS` (the only suite list)

Explicit list of `(file_stem, generate_fn)` — **no** dynamic `ALL_GENERATORS` / `_collect()`.
Stem → `tests/priv/SvH/SvH_<stem>-00.S`. Counts: twostage **7** + csr **3** + perm **13** + fault **3** + misc **13** = **39**.

### 2.3 `make_svh` loop

For each scenario: open chunk → banner → reset `sigupd_count` / `num_testcases` → `chunk.code.extend(generate_fn(…))` → floor `num_testcases` at 1 → `end_test_chunk()`.

---

## 3. Shared library — `SvHCommon.py` (what almost every test calls)

### 3.1 Fixed addresses (must match coverpoint bins + env macros)

| Symbol | Value | Role |
|--------|-------|------|
| `VA_CODE` | `0x90000000` | Guest VA of relocated VS/VU code |
| `VA_DATA_SV32` / `VA_DATA_SV39` | `0x008001000` / `0x0000000080010000` | Guest data VA |
| `GPA_CODE_*` / `GPA_DATA_*` | see Common | G-stage inputs |
| `GPA_VROOT_*` / `GPA_VLVL0_*` / `GPA_VLVL1_SV39` | see Common | GPAs of VS page-table pages |
| `PTE_CODE_G` / `PTE_DATA_G` | U=1 | G-stage leaves (walked as user) |
| `PTE_CODE_VS` / `PTE_DATA_VS` | U=0 | VS leaves for VS-mode guests |
| `CG` | `"SvH_cg"` | Covergroup tag in SIGUPD metadata |

### 3.2 `build_twins` — why `max`, not `sum`

1. Save counters → run `asm_for_paging(td, "sv32")` → save counts.  
2. Rewind counters → run `"sv39"`.  
3. Set counters to `max(sv32, sv39)`.  
4. Return `#ifdef SV32_SUPPORTED` … `#ifdef SV39_SUPPORTED`.

Only one twin survives preprocess, so `SIGUPD_COUNT` must be the larger twin.

### 3.3 Canonical twin body order

```text
set_va_gpa_symbols(paging)
build_two_stage_maps(...)          # emits G_PTE_SETUP / VS_PTE_SETUP / V_SAVE_AREA_SETUP
enable_two_stage / enable_vs_only / disable_paging
goto_vs / goto_vu / goto_hs
  stimulus
goto_mmode                         # signature page not guest-mapped
sigupd_* + count_sigupds
twin_data(...)                     # test_region, page tables, mismatch .string
```

`two_stage_setup(paging)` packs symbols + maps + enable.

### 3.4 Mode hops (Python → macro)

| Helper | Typical asm | When |
|--------|-------------|------|
| `goto_vs` / `goto_vu` / `goto_hs` | `RVTEST_GOTO_LOWER_MODE VSmode/…` | VA≠PA relocated guests |
| Bare hop | `RVTEST_TSBI_GOTO_VSMODE` (emitted raw in some bodies) | Identity addressing |
| `goto_mmode` | `RVTEST_GOTO_MMODE` | Before SIGUPD |
| `enable_two_stage` | `VSATP_SETUP` + `HGATP_SETUP` + both hfence | Two-stage ON |
| `enable_vs_only` | `VSATP_SETUP(..., PA)` + `csrw hgatp,x0` | G Bare |
| `disable_paging` | clear both ATPs | Both Bare |

**MISSING DEPENDENCY:** macro bodies in `tests/env/*hypervisor*` / arch-test headers — not defined in `SvH*.py`.

---

## 4. How to read each per-test chapter below

Every chapter uses the same template:

1. **What / Why / How** — plain three-line summary.  
2. **Coverpoint map** — which `SvH_cg` bin(s) this ELF hits.  
3. **Wire-up** — `_SCENARIOS` stem → `generate_*` → body `_asm_*` → twin finisher.  
4. **CSR / mode timeline** — vsatp, hgatp, privilege hops.  
5. **Body Python highlights** — live line refs from `SvH*.py`.  
6. **Generated assembly spine** — excerpt from the `.S` file.  
7. **Why those instructions (mapping rules)** — per-test flow: which Python helper / asm idiom maps to which spine insn (**different for each test**).

Global env/Sail/coverage list: **§7 Cross-cutting missing dependencies** (once, not repeated 39×).

Twostage tests often have **no** `// Test case N:` banners (labeled SIGUPDs). Perm/fault/misc usually have numbered case tables.

---

## 5. Worked master example — `two_stage_rw_VSmode` (read this first)

### Wire-up

- `_SCENARIOS` entry: `("two_stage_rw_VSmode", generate_two_stage_rw_VSmode)` in `SvH.py`.  
- `generate_two_stage_rw_VSmode` (`SvH_twostage.py`) → `comment_banner` + `build_twins(td, _asm_two_stage_rw)`.  
- Output: `tests/priv/SvH/SvH_two_stage_rw_VSmode-00.S`, `SIGUPD_COUNT` includes margin + **2** dumps/twin.

### `_asm_two_stage_rw` step-by-step

| Step | Python | Emitted idea | Why |
|------|--------|--------------|-----|
| 1 | `two_stage_setup(paging)` | `.set va_*` / `gpa_*`; `G_PTE_SETUP`/`VS_PTE_SETUP`; `VSATP_SETUP`+`HGATP_SETUP`; hfence | Map `va_data→gpa_data→test_region` and `va_code→gpa_code→code` |
| 2 | `preload_spa(0xDEADBEEF)` | `LI` + `la test_region` + `sw` | Known SPA pattern before guest store |
| 3 | `load_guest_va()` | `LI(a5, va_data)` | Guest pointer |
| 4 | `goto_vs()` | `RVTEST_GOTO_LOWER_MODE VSmode` | Run under VS with VA≠PA |
| 5 | raw `LI(a2,…)` / `sw` / `lw a3` | Store then load through **both** walks | Hits VS then G |
| 6 | `goto_mmode()` | `RVTEST_GOTO_MMODE` | Signature not guest-mapped |
| 7 | `sigupd_labeled("test1_vs","a3")` | `RVTEST_SIGUPD(..., a3, …)` | Capture guest load |
| 8 | SPA `lw t2` + `sigupd_labeled("test2_spa","t2")` | Prove store reached SPA | Not leftover `0xDEADBEEF` |
| 9 | `count_sigupds(td, 2)` + `twin_data` | Header budget + `.data` | Mismatch strings for fail text |

### CPU walk (conceptual)

1. M builds G and VS tables (macros expand to PTE stores).  
2. `vsatp` ON (PPNs are **GPAs**), `hgatp` ON (`sv32x4`/`sv39x4`), TLB fences.  
3. VS `sw`/`lw` at `va_data` walk VS→GPA→G→SPA.  
4. M SIGUPD guest GPR then SPA word.  
5. Later: ELF → Sail → `.rvvi` → `SvH_cg` bins for two-stage R/W.

### Live `.S` stimulus (Sv32 twin, abbreviated)

```asm
  VSATP_SETUP(sv32, GPA)
  HGATP_SETUP(sv32x4)
  hfence.vvma
  hfence.gvma
  LI(t0, 0xdeadbeef)
  la t1, test_region
  sw t0, 0(t1)
  LI(a5, va_data)
  RVTEST_GOTO_LOWER_MODE VSmode
  LI(a2, 0x257A0005)
  sw   a2, 0(a5)
  lw   a3, 0(a5)
  RVTEST_GOTO_MMODE
  RVTEST_SIGUPD(..., a3, test1_vs, ...)
  la t1, test_region
  lw t2, 0(t1)
  RVTEST_SIGUPD(..., t2, test2_spa, ...)
```

## 6. Per-test chapters (all 39)

### Family A — `SvH_twostage.py` (paging on/off, ifetch, MXR)

Shared pattern: `generate_*` → `build_twins` (or `_finish_twin_test` / `_wrap_sv39_only`) → `_asm_*`. Each test ends with **Why those instructions (mapping rules)** — test-specific flow, not copy-pasted boilerplate.
#### T01. `g_walk_vs_pt_VSmode` → `SvH_g_walk_vs_pt_VSmode-00.S`
- **What:** A generated RISC-V Hypervisor test that checks G-stage translation of VS page-table pages in VS-mode (first allow, then deny).
- **Why:** To verify that the processor walks VS page tables through G-stage and faults when that G-stage mapping is invalid, for coverage.
- **How:** It sets up two-stage paging, does a VS-mode load that succeeds, then makes the G-stage PTE of the VS page table invalid, retries the load, checks the results through the signature mechanism, and records coverage.
- **Coverpoint map:** `cp_h_vm_gstagetrans` (`SvH_cg`)
- **Python entry:** `generate_g_walk_vs_pt_VSmode` in `SvH_twostage.py` lines **300-307**.
- **Docstring / intent:** Write SvH_g_walk_vs_pt_VSmode-00.S (G-stage walk of VS page tables).
- **Body builder:** `_asm_g_walk_vs_pt` lines **255-299** (one paging twin).
- **Twin / wrap:** helpers used: `build_twins, comment_banner, count_sigupds, fence_both_stages, goto_mmode, goto_vs, load_guest_va, mode_names, preload_spa, pte_bits, sigupd_labeled, twin_data, two_stage_setup`. Finisher is `build_twins` unless this is misc `_finish_*` or csr `_wrap_sv39_only`.
- **Generated header:** `#define SIGUPD_COUNT 12` in `tests/priv/SvH/SvH_g_walk_vs_pt_VSmode-00.S` (includes writer margin + body dumps; twin uses **max** not sum).
- **CSR / mode timeline:**
  - Two-stage maps (G + VS); `vsatp` and `hgatp` programmed unless later overridden.
  - Stimulus privilege: **VS** (`RVTEST_GOTO_LOWER_MODE VSmode`).
  - Return **M** before SIGUPD (`RVTEST_GOTO_MMODE`).
- **Cases:** no `// Test case N:` banners — directed labeled SIGUPD flow (see stimulus list).
- **Body Python highlights (live lines, abbreviated):**
  ```python
  # L255: def _asm_g_walk_vs_pt(test_data: TestData, paging: Paging) -> list[str]:  # define _asm_g_walk_vs_pt
  # L258:     lines = two_stage_setup(paging)  # includes G leaf of VS L0 table
  # L259:     lines.extend(preload_spa(0x257A0041))  # known pattern for allow-path load
  # L260:     lines.extend(load_guest_va())  # a5 = va_data
  # L261:     lines.extend(goto_vs())  # enter VS for allow-path lw
  # L262:     lines.extend(
  # L264:             "  lw   a3, 0(a5)",  # G allows VS-PT walk → load completes
  # L268:     lines.extend(goto_mmode())  # back to M after allow path
  # L269:     lines.extend(sigupd_labeled("test1_allow", "a3"))  # dump allow-path load
  # L270:     deny = pte_bits(x=False, w=True, r=True, u=True, v=False)  # G leaf V=0 on VS L0 page
  # L271:     lines.append(f"  G_PTE_SETUP({g_mode}, rvtest_vlvl0_pg_tbl, {deny}, gpa_rvtest_vlvl0_pg_tbl, LEVEL0)")  # invalidate G leaf over VS L0
  # L272:     lines.extend(fence_both_stages())  # flush both TLBs after PTE change
  # L273:     lines.extend(
  # L275:             "  LI(a4, 0)",  # fault sentinel before deny-path load
  # L278:     lines.extend(goto_vs())  # re-enter VS for deny-path lw
  # L279:     lines.extend(
  # L281:             "  lw   a4, 0(a5)",  # guest-page fault; a4 stays 0
  # L285:     lines.extend(goto_mmode())  # back to M after deny path
  # L286:     lines.extend(sigupd_labeled("test2_deny", "a4"))  # dump deny-path sentinel
  # L287:     count_sigupds(test_data, 2)  # two SIGUPD sites
  # L288:     lines.extend(
  # L289:         twin_data(
  # L291:             mismatch_strings=[  # fail messages for each SIGUPD
  # L292:                 mismatch_string("test1_allow", "Mismatch on VS lw with G allowing VS-PT walk"),  # allow fail text
  # L293:                 mismatch_string("test2_deny", "Mismatch on VS lw with G denying VS-PT walk"),  # deny fail text
  # L297:     return lines  # full twin asm for G walk of VS PTs
  ```
- **Generated assembly spine (first live twin; setup macros + stimulus + SIGUPD):**
  ```asm
  .set va_code,                 0x90000000
  .set gpa_code,                0x12000000
  .set gpa_rvtest_Vroot_pg_tbl, 0x1000a000
  .set gpa_rvtest_vlvl0_pg_tbl, 0x1000b000
  .set va_data,                 0x8001000
  .set gpa_data,                0xc002000
  SUPERPAGE_G_PTE_SETUP(sv32x4, rvtest_code_begin, (PTE_D | PTE_A | PTE_U | PTE_X | PTE_R | PTE_V), gpa_code, LEVEL1)
  G_PTE_SETUP(sv32x4, rvtest_hlvl0_pg_tbl, (PTE_V), gpa_rvtest_Vroot_pg_tbl, LEVEL1)
  G_PTE_SETUP(sv32x4, rvtest_Vroot_pg_tbl, (PTE_D | PTE_A | PTE_U | PTE_W | PTE_R | PTE_V), gpa_rvtest_Vroot_pg_tbl, LEVEL0)
  G_PTE_SETUP(sv32x4, rvtest_vlvl0_pg_tbl, (PTE_D | PTE_A | PTE_U | PTE_W | PTE_R | PTE_V), gpa_rvtest_vlvl0_pg_tbl, LEVEL0)
  G_PTE_SETUP(sv32x4, rvtest_hlvl0_pg_tbl, (PTE_V), gpa_data, LEVEL1)
  G_PTE_SETUP(sv32x4, test_region, (PTE_D | PTE_A | PTE_U | PTE_W | PTE_R | PTE_V), gpa_data, LEVEL0)
  VS_PTE_SETUP(sv32, GPA, gpa_code, (PTE_D | PTE_A | PTE_X | PTE_R | PTE_V), va_code, LEVEL1)
  V_SAVE_AREA_SETUP(va_code, rvtest_code_begin, code, LEVEL1)
  VS_PTE_SETUP(sv32, GPA, gpa_rvtest_vlvl0_pg_tbl, (PTE_V), va_data, LEVEL1)
  VS_PTE_SETUP(sv32, GPA, gpa_data, (PTE_D | PTE_A | PTE_W | PTE_R | PTE_V), va_data, LEVEL0)
  VSATP_SETUP(sv32, GPA)
  HGATP_SETUP(sv32x4)
  hfence.vvma
  hfence.gvma
  LI(t0, 0x257a0041)
  la t1, test_region
  sw t0, 0(t1)
  LI(a5, va_data)
  RVTEST_GOTO_LOWER_MODE VSmode
  lw   a3, 0(a5)
  nop
  RVTEST_GOTO_MMODE
  RVTEST_SIGUPD(x2, x5, x4, a3, test1_allow, test1_allow_str)
  G_PTE_SETUP(sv32x4, rvtest_vlvl0_pg_tbl, (PTE_D | PTE_A | PTE_U | PTE_W | PTE_R), gpa_rvtest_vlvl0_pg_tbl, LEVEL0)
  hfence.vvma
  hfence.gvma
  LI(a4, 0)
  RVTEST_GOTO_LOWER_MODE VSmode
  lw   a4, 0(a5)
  nop
  RVTEST_GOTO_MMODE
  RVTEST_SIGUPD(x2, x5, x4, a4, test2_deny, test2_deny_str)
  rvtest_hlvl0_pg_tbl:
  test1_allow_str: .string "\"Mismatch on VS lw with G allowing VS-PT walk\""
  test2_deny_str: .string "\"Mismatch on VS lw with G denying VS-PT walk\""
  ```
- **Why those instructions (mapping rules):**
  - `two_stage_setup` → `.set va_*`/`gpa_*` plus G+VS PTE walk with **G leaf covering VS L0 table GPA**.
  - `preload_spa` → M-mode SPA seed; allow-path VS `lw a3`; SIGUPD `test1_allow`.
  - `pte_bits` + `G_PTE_SETUP` (V=0 on VS L0) + fences → deny-path `lw a4` faults; SIGUPD `test2_deny`.


---

#### T02. `hgatp_bare_trans_VSmode` → `SvH_hgatp_bare_trans_VSmode-00.S`
- **What:** A generated RISC-V Hypervisor test that checks VS-stage load/store when `hgatp` is Bare (G-stage off).
- **Why:** To verify that VS-mode paging works on its own when `hgatp` is zero, and that `hgatp` stays zero, for coverage.
- **How:** It maps VS pages to physical addresses, clears `hgatp`, enters VS-mode, stores then loads, reads `hgatp` back, checks the results through the signature mechanism, and records coverage.
- **Coverpoint map:** `cp_hgatp_bare_trans` (`SvH_cg`)
- **Python entry:** `generate_hgatp_bare_trans_VSmode` in `SvH_twostage.py` lines **247-254**.
- **Docstring / intent:** Write SvH_hgatp_bare_trans_VSmode-00.S (VS paging, hgatp Bare).
- **Body builder:** `_asm_hgatp_bare` lines **202-246** (one paging twin).
- **Twin / wrap:** helpers used: `_vs_only_page_tables, build_twins, comment_banner, count_sigupds, goto_mmode, goto_vs, load_guest_va, preload_spa, set_va_gpa_symbols, sigupd_labeled, twin_data`. Finisher is `build_twins` unless this is misc `_finish_*` or csr `_wrap_sv39_only`.
- **Generated header:** `#define SIGUPD_COUNT 13` in `tests/priv/SvH/SvH_hgatp_bare_trans_VSmode-00.S` (includes writer margin + body dumps; twin uses **max** not sum).
- **CSR / mode timeline:**
  - VS-stage ON with **PA** leaf PPNs; `hgatp` Bare.
  - Stimulus privilege: **VS** (`RVTEST_GOTO_LOWER_MODE VSmode`).
  - Return **M** before SIGUPD (`RVTEST_GOTO_MMODE`).
- **Cases:** no `// Test case N:` banners — directed labeled SIGUPD flow (see stimulus list).
- **Body Python highlights (live lines, abbreviated):**
  ```python
  # L202: def _asm_hgatp_bare(test_data: TestData, paging: Paging) -> list[str]:  # define _asm_hgatp_bare
  # L204:     lines = set_va_gpa_symbols(paging)  # .set va_* / gpa_* for this twin
  # L205:     lines.extend(_vs_only_page_tables(paging))  # VS maps with PA PPNs
  # L206:     lines.extend(preload_spa(0xDEADBEEF))  # known pattern in the physical page
  # L207:     lines.extend(load_guest_va())  # a5 = va_data
  # L208:     lines.extend(goto_vs())  # enter VS (VA-relocated)
  # L209:     lines.extend(
  # L211:             "  LI(a2, 0x257A0005)",  # store payload
  # L212:             "  sw   a2, 0(a5)",  # VS-stage only (hgatp Bare)
  # L214:             "  lw   a3, 0(a5)",  # VS load; a3 must match a2
  # L218:     lines.extend(goto_mmode())  # back to M for signature
  # L219:     lines.extend(sigupd_labeled("test1_vs", "a3"))  # dump guest load result
  # L220:     lines.extend(
  # L223:             "  lw t2, 0(t1)",  # SPA readback of stored word
  # L226:     lines.extend(sigupd_labeled("test2_spa", "t2"))  # dump SPA word
  # L227:     lines.extend(
  # L229:             "  test3_hgatp:",  # label for hgatp SIGUPD case
  # L233:     count_sigupds(test_data, 3)  # lw + SPA + hgatp
  # L234:     lines.extend(
  # L235:         twin_data(
  # L237:             mismatch_strings=[  # fail messages for each SIGUPD
  # L238:                 mismatch_string("test1_vs", "Mismatch on VS lw (hgatp Bare)"),  # guest load fail text
  # L239:                 mismatch_string("test2_spa", "Mismatch on SPA readback (hgatp Bare)"),  # SPA fail text
  # L240:                 mismatch_string("test3_hgatp", "Mismatch on hgatp still Bare"),  # hgatp fail text
  # L244:     return lines  # full twin asm for VS-on / G-Bare
  ```
- **Generated assembly spine (first live twin; setup macros + stimulus + SIGUPD):**
  ```asm
  .set va_code,                 0x90000000
  .set gpa_code,                0x12000000
  .set gpa_rvtest_Vroot_pg_tbl, 0x1000a000
  .set gpa_rvtest_vlvl0_pg_tbl, 0x1000b000
  .set va_data,                 0x8001000
  .set gpa_data,                0xc002000
  SUPERPAGE_VS_PTE_SETUP(sv32, rvtest_code_begin, (PTE_D | PTE_A | PTE_X | PTE_R | PTE_V), va_code, LEVEL1)
  V_SAVE_AREA_SETUP(va_code, rvtest_code_begin, code, LEVEL1)
  VS_PTE_SETUP(sv32, PA, rvtest_vlvl0_pg_tbl, (PTE_V), va_data, LEVEL1)
  VS_PTE_SETUP(sv32, PA, test_region, (PTE_D | PTE_A | PTE_W | PTE_R | PTE_V), va_data, LEVEL0)
  ```

#### T07. `two_stage_rw_VSmode` → `SvH_two_stage_rw_VSmode-00.S`
- **What:** A generated RISC-V Hypervisor test that checks two-stage load and store in VS-mode.
- **Why:** To verify that a VS-mode store and load walk both VS-stage and G-stage and land in real physical memory, for coverage.
- **How:** It sets up two-stage paging, enters VS-mode, stores then loads a known value, reads the physical page back from M-mode, checks the results through the signature mechanism, and records coverage.
- **Coverpoint map:** `two_stage_read / two_stage_write (generator: cp_two_stage_rw)` (`SvH_cg`)
- **Python entry:** `generate_two_stage_rw_VSmode` in `SvH_twostage.py` lines **110-117**.
- **Docstring / intent:** Write SvH_two_stage_rw_VSmode-00.S (cp_two_stage_rw).
- **Body builder:** `_asm_two_stage_rw` lines **73-109** (one paging twin).
- **Twin / wrap:** helpers used: `build_twins, comment_banner, count_sigupds, goto_mmode, goto_vs, load_guest_va, preload_spa, sigupd_labeled, twin_data, two_stage_setup`. Finisher is `build_twins` unless this is misc `_finish_*` or csr `_wrap_sv39_only`.
- **Generated header:** `#define SIGUPD_COUNT 12` in `tests/priv/SvH/SvH_two_stage_rw_VSmode-00.S` (includes writer margin + body dumps; twin uses **max** not sum).
- **CSR / mode timeline:**
  - Two-stage maps (G + VS); `vsatp` and `hgatp` programmed unless later overridden.
  - Stimulus privilege: **VS** (`RVTEST_GOTO_LOWER_MODE VSmode`).
  - Return **M** before SIGUPD (`RVTEST_GOTO_MMODE`).
- **Cases:** no `// Test case N:` banners — directed labeled SIGUPD flow (see stimulus list).
- **Body Python highlights (live lines, abbreviated):**

  ```python
  # L75:     lines = two_stage_setup(paging)  # .set, G+VS maps, vsatp+hgatp, fences

  # L77:     lines.extend(load_guest_va())  # a5 = va_data

  # L84:             "  lw   a3, 0(a5)",  # VS load; a3 must match a2
  # L88:     lines.extend(goto_mmode())  # signature is not guest-mapped
  # L89:     lines.extend(sigupd_labeled("test1_vs", "a3"))  # dump guest load result

  # L97:     count_sigupds(test_data, 2)  # two SIGUPD sites

  # L99:         twin_data(
  # L101:             mismatch_strings=[  # fail messages for each SIGUPD
  # L102:                 mismatch_string("test1_vs", "Mismatch on VS lw after two-stage sw"),  # guest load fail text
  # L103:                 mismatch_string("test2_spa", "Mismatch on SPA readback after two-stage store"),  # SPA fail text
  # L107:     return lines  # full twin asm for two-stage sw/lw
  ```

### Family B — `SvH_csr.py` (ATP CSR field walks)

HS (or VS for satp) WARL/MODE/PPN/VMID-ASID stimulus. Mostly `csrw` + CSR SIGUPD; little or no guest data access. `csr_satp_mode_VSmode` is **Sv39-only** (`_wrap_sv39_only`), not `build_twins`.
#### T08. `csr_hgatp_fields_HSmode` → `SvH_csr_hgatp_fields_HSmode-00.S`
- **What:** A generated RISC-V Hypervisor test that checks the `hgatp` MODE, VMID, and PPN fields in HS-mode, including MODE values `0–15`.
- **Why:** To verify that the processor correctly legalizes `hgatp` encodings and reports the expected results for coverage.
- **How:** It enters HS-mode, writes MODE values `0–15`, VMID all-ones, and walking-1 PPN bits into `hgatp`, reads them back, checks the results through the signature mechanism, and records coverage.
- **Coverpoint map:** `cp_hgatp_mode_field, cp_hgatp_ppn_field, cp_hgatp_vmidlen_detect, cp_hgatp_vmid_scoping` (`SvH_cg`)
- **Python entry:** `generate_csr_hgatp_fields_HSmode` in `SvH_csr.py` lines **448-455**.
- **Docstring / intent:** Output ``SvH_csr_hgatp_fields_HSmode-00.S``.
- **Body builder:** `_asm_hgatp` lines **154-256** (one paging twin).
- **Twin / wrap:** helpers used: `build_twins, comment_banner, goto_mmode, mode_names`. Finisher is `build_twins` unless this is misc `_finish_*` or csr `_wrap_sv39_only`.
- **Generated header:** `#define SIGUPD_COUNT 89` in `tests/priv/SvH/SvH_csr_hgatp_fields_HSmode-00.S` (includes writer margin + body dumps; twin uses **max** not sum).
- **CSR / mode timeline:**
  - Return **M** before SIGUPD (`RVTEST_GOTO_MMODE`).
- **Cases:** no `// Test case N:` banners — directed labeled SIGUPD flow (see stimulus list).
- **Body Python highlights (live lines, abbreviated):**
  ```python
  # L154: def _asm_hgatp(test_data: TestData, paging: Paging) -> list[str]:  # HS hgatp field stimulus
  # L155:     """HS-mode hgatp MODE, VMID, and PPN field stimulus for one paging twin."""
  # L158:     shift = 31 if is_sv32 else 60  # MODE field bit position in hgatp
  # L159:     mode_const = f"HGATP_MODE_{hgatp_mode.upper()}"  # assembler symbol for legal MODE
  # L160:     ppn_bits = 22 if is_sv32 else 44  # exclusive upper bound for walking-1
  # L162:     ppn_ones = "0x3fffff" if is_sv32 else f"0x{(((1 << 44) - 1) & ~0x3):x}"  # all writable PPN bits
  # L163:     vmid_mask = "HGATP32_VMID" if is_sv32 else "HGATP64_VMID"  # all-ones VMID field
  # L164:     suffix = "sv32" if is_sv32 else "sv39"  # bin-name suffix so twins do not collide
  # L167:     lines = [  # prelude before case banners
  # L168:         "  csrw vsatp, x0                                // VS Bare; isolate hgatp field bins",  # clear VS paging
  # L169:         "  hfence.vvma                                   // flush VS-stage TLB after vsatp clear",  # TLB sync
  # L172:     lines.extend(_section("Test Cases Start from here"))  # case list begins
  # L173:     if is_sv32:  # RV32: explicit Bare sample before Sv32x4
  # L174:         lines.extend(_case("Set hgatp.MODE=Bare", "cp_hgatp_mode_field"))  # Bare case banner
  # L175:         lines.extend(  # Bare write + SIGUPD
  # L176:             _write_csr_then_sigupd(  # write CSR then SIGUPD
  # L179:                 value=None,  # csrw hgatp, x0
  # L180:                 bin_name="mode_bare_sv32",  # Bare bin for Sv32 twin
  # L185:     lines.extend(_case(f"Set hgatp.MODE={hgatp_mode}, PPN=0", "cp_hgatp_mode_field"))  # legal MODE banner
  # L186:     lines.extend(  # legal MODE, PPN=0 sample
  # L187:         _write_csr_then_sigupd(  # write CSR then SIGUPD
  # L196:     if not is_sv32:  # RV64: WARL walk of MODE 0..15 plus unsupported MODE=1
  # L197:         lines.extend(  # full MODE WARL walks
  # L208:         lines.extend(_case("Set hgatp.MODE=1 sample", "cp_hgatp_mode_field"))  # unsupported-1 banner
  # L209:         lines.extend(  # MODE=1 sample
  # L210:             _write_csr_then_sigupd(  # write CSR then SIGUPD
  # L219:     lines.extend(_case("hgatp.VMID = all ones", "cp_hgatp_vmidlen_detect cp_hgatp_vmid_scoping"))  # VMID banner
  # L220:     for readback in ("vmidlen", "scoping"):  # two SIGUPD of the same write (two coverpoints)
  # L221:         lines.extend(  # all-ones VMID write + dump
  # L222:             _write_csr_then_sigupd(  # write CSR then SIGUPD
  # L227:                 coverpoint="cp_hgatp_vmidlen_detect" if readback == "vmidlen" else "cp_hgatp_vmid_scoping",  # pick cp
  # L228:                 note=f"write all VMID bits for {readback} readback",  # // note
  # L231:     lines.extend(_case("hgatp.PPN = all ones", "cp_hgatp_ppn_field"))  # PPN all-ones banner
  # L232:     lines.extend(  # PPN all-ones sample
  # L233:         _write_csr_then_sigupd(  # write CSR then SIGUPD
  # L242:     lines.extend(  # walking-1 on PPN
  # L253:     lines.extend(goto_mmode())  # SIGUPD of hgatp already ran in HS; return to M
  # L254:     return lines  # full hgatp twin body
  ```
- **Generated assembly spine (first live twin; setup macros + stimulus + SIGUPD):**
  ```asm
  csrw vsatp, x0                                // VS Bare; isolate hgatp field bins
  hfence.vvma                                   // flush VS-stage TLB after vsatp clear
  RVTEST_TSBI_GOTO_SMODE                       // M->HS (T-SBI)
  // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  //--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // Set hgatp.MODE=Bare | Expected: Successful
  //--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  csrw hgatp, x0                                // write Bare MODE
  RVTEST_SIGUPD_CSR_READ(hgatp, x14, SvH_cg_cp_hgatp_mode_field_mode_bare_sv32, SvH_cg_cp_hgatp_mode_field_mode_bare_sv32_str)
  //--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // Set hgatp.MODE=sv32x4, PPN=0 | Expected: Successful
  //--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  LI(t0, (HGATP_MODE_SV32X4 << 31))
  csrw hgatp, t0                                // write sv32x4 with PPN=0
  RVTEST_SIGUPD_CSR_READ(hgatp, x14, SvH_cg_cp_hgatp_mode_field_mode_sv32x4_sv32, SvH_cg_cp_hgatp_mode_field_mode_sv32x4_sv32_str)
  //--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // hgatp.VMID = all ones | Expected: Successful
  //--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  LI(t0, (HGATP_MODE_SV32X4 << 31) | HGATP32_VMID)
  csrw hgatp, t0                                // write all VMID bits for vmidlen readback
  RVTEST_SIGUPD_CSR_READ(hgatp, x14, SvH_cg_cp_hgatp_vmidlen_detect_vmidlen_sv32, SvH_cg_cp_hgatp_vmidlen_detect_vmidlen_sv32_str)
  LI(t0, (HGATP_MODE_SV32X4 << 31) | HGATP32_VMID)
  csrw hgatp, t0                                // write all VMID bits for scoping readback
  RVTEST_SIGUPD_CSR_READ(hgatp, x14, SvH_cg_cp_hgatp_vmid_scoping_scoping_sv32, SvH_cg_cp_hgatp_vmid_scoping_scoping_sv32_str)
  //--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // hgatp.PPN = all ones | Expected: Successful
  //--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  csrw hgatp, t0                                // write PPN all-ones pattern
  RVTEST_SIGUPD_CSR_READ(hgatp, x14, SvH_cg_cp_hgatp_ppn_field_ppn_ones_sv32, SvH_cg_cp_hgatp_ppn_field_ppn_ones_sv32_str)
  //--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  RVTEST_SIGUPD_CSR_READ(hgatp, x14, SvH_cg_cp_hgatp_ppn_field_sv32_ppn_bit_02, SvH_cg_cp_hgatp_ppn_field_sv32_ppn_bit_02_str)
  LI(t0, (HGATP_MODE_SV32X4 << 31) | 0x8)

  LI(t0, (HGATP_MODE_SV32X4 << 31) | 0x10)
  csrw hgatp, t0                                // write walking-1 PPN bit 4
  RVTEST_SIGUPD_CSR_READ(hgatp, x14, SvH_cg_cp_hgatp_ppn_field_sv32_ppn_bit_04, SvH_cg_cp_hgatp_ppn_field_sv32_ppn_bit_04_str)
  LI(t0, (HGATP_MODE_SV32X4 << 31) | 0x20)
  csrw hgatp, t0                                // write walking-1 PPN bit 5
  RVTEST_SIGUPD_CSR_READ(hgatp, x14, SvH_cg_cp_hgatp_ppn_field_sv32_ppn_bit_05, SvH_cg_cp_hgatp_ppn_field_sv32_ppn_bit_05_str)
  LI(t0, (HGATP_MODE_SV32X4 << 31) | 0x40)

  RVTEST_SIGUPD_CSR_READ(hgatp, x14, SvH_cg_cp_hgatp_ppn_field_sv32_ppn_bit_06, SvH_cg_cp_hgatp_ppn_field_sv32_ppn_bit_06_str)
  ```

#### T09. `csr_satp_mode_VSmode` → `SvH_csr_satp_mode_VSmode-00.S`
- **What:** A generated RISC-V Hypervisor test that checks the `satp.MODE` field in VS-mode, including `satp`/`vsatp` behavior and MODE values `0–15`.
- **Why:** To verify that the processor correctly handles all possible `satp.MODE` encodings and reports the expected results for coverage.
- **How:** It sets up VS-mode with Sv39, reads `satp`/`vsatp`, writes MODE values `0–15` into `satp`, reads them back, checks the results through the signature mechanism, and records coverage.

  ```asm
  .set va_code, 0x0000000080000000              // GVA where VS executes this test code
  VS_PTE_SETUP(sv39, PA, rvtest_code_begin, (PTE_D | PTE_A | PTE_X | PTE_R | PTE_W | PTE_V), va_code, LEVEL2)
  V_SAVE_AREA_SETUP(va_code, rvtest_code_begin, code, LEVEL2)
  VSATP_SETUP(sv39, PA)                         // vsatp ON; hgatp stays Bare
  csrw hgatp, x0
  hfence.vvma
  hfence.gvma
  // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  //--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // Read satp/vsatp alias with MODE=Sv39 | Expected: Successful
  //--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  RVTEST_GOTO_LOWER_MODE VSmode                 // M->VS (VA!=PA relocate)
  RVTEST_GOTO_MMODE
  RVTEST_SIGUPD(x2, x5, x4, x13, SvH_cg_cp_satp_mode_field_satp_alias_sv39, SvH_cg_cp_satp_mode_field_satp_alias_sv39_str)
  RVTEST_SIGUPD(x2, x5, x4, x14, SvH_cg_cp_satp_mode_field_vsatp_alias_sv39, SvH_cg_cp_satp_mode_field_vsatp_alias_sv39_str)
  //--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // Walk satp.MODE 0..15 starting Bare | Expected: Successful
  //--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  RVTEST_GOTO_LOWER_MODE VSmode
  csrw satp, x0
  LI(t3, 0)
  csrw satp, x0
  csrw satp, t0
  csrw satp, x0                                 // restore Bare before M hop
  RVTEST_GOTO_MMODE
  RVTEST_SIGUPD(x2, x5, x4, x15, SvH_cg_cp_satp_mode_field_bare_mode_walk, SvH_cg_cp_satp_mode_field_bare_mode_walk_str)
  RVTEST_GOTO_LOWER_MODE VSmode
  addi t3, t3, 1
  LI(t4, 16)
  RVTEST_GOTO_MMODE
  //--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // Walk satp.MODE 0..15 starting Sv39 | Expected: Successful
  //--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  RVTEST_GOTO_LOWER_MODE VSmode
  li   t0, ~((0xF) << 60)
  li   t0, (SATP_MODE_SV39 << 60)
  csrw satp, t1
  LI(t3, 0)
  csrw satp, t1
  li   t0, (SATP_MODE_SV39 << 60)
  csrw satp, t1                                 // restore Sv39 before M hop
  RVTEST_GOTO_MMODE
  RVTEST_SIGUPD(x2, x5, x4, x15, SvH_cg_cp_satp_mode_field_sv39_mode_walk, SvH_cg_cp_satp_mode_field_sv39_mode_walk_str)
  RVTEST_GOTO_LOWER_MODE VSmode
  li   t0, (SATP_MODE_SV39 << 60)
  csrw satp, t1
  addi t3, t3, 1
  LI(t4, 16)
  RVTEST_GOTO_MMODE
  //--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // Final satp read after MODE walks | Expected: Successful
  //--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  RVTEST_GOTO_LOWER_MODE VSmode
  RVTEST_GOTO_MMODE
  RVTEST_SIGUPD(x2, x5, x4, x16, SvH_cg_cp_satp_mode_field_final_satp_sv39, SvH_cg_cp_satp_mode_field_final_satp_sv39_str)
  ```

  - CSR `csrw satp` + SIGUPD — not guest lw/sw; RV64/Sv39 twin only.

#### T10. `csr_vsatp_fields_HSmode` → `SvH_csr_vsatp_fields_HSmode-00.S`
- **What:** A generated RISC-V Hypervisor test that checks the `vsatp` MODE, ASID, and PPN fields in HS-mode, including MODE values `0–15`.
- **Why:** To verify that the processor correctly legalizes `vsatp` encodings and reports the expected results for coverage.

  ```asm
  hfence.gvma                                   // flush old G-stage TLB after hgatp clear
  RVTEST_TSBI_GOTO_SMODE                       // M->HS (T-SBI)
  // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  //--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // Set vsatp.MODE=sv32, PPN=0 | Expected: Successful
  //--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  LI(t0, (SATP_MODE_SV32 << 31))
  csrw vsatp, t0                                // write sv32 with PPN=0
  RVTEST_SIGUPD_CSR_READ(vsatp, x14, SvH_cg_cp_vsatp_ppn_field_mode_sv32_sv32, SvH_cg_cp_vsatp_ppn_field_mode_sv32_sv32_str)
  //--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // vsatp.ASID = all ones | Expected: Successful
  //--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  LI(t0, (SATP_MODE_SV32 << 31) | SATP32_ASID)
  csrw vsatp, t0                                // write all ASID bits
  RVTEST_SIGUPD_CSR_READ(vsatp, x14, SvH_cg_cp_vsatp_asidlen_detect_asid_ones_sv32, SvH_cg_cp_vsatp_asidlen_detect_asid_ones_sv32_str)
  //--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // vsatp.PPN = all ones | Expected: Successful

  csrw vsatp, t0                                // write PPN all-ones pattern

  //--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // Walking ones on vsatp.PPN bits [21:0] | Expected: Successful

  LI(t0, (SATP_MODE_SV32 << 31) | 0x8)

  RVTEST_SIGUPD_CSR_READ(vsatp, x14, SvH_cg_cp_vsatp_ppn_field_sv32_ppn_bit_03, SvH_cg_cp_vsatp_ppn_field_sv32_ppn_bit_03_str)
  LI(t0, (SATP_MODE_SV32 << 31) | 0x10)
  ```

#### T11. `g_perm_VSmode` → `SvH_g_perm_VSmode-00.S`
- **What:** A generated RISC-V Hypervisor test that checks G-stage page permissions from VS-mode, plus HS-mode HLV/HSV.
- **Why:** To verify that G-stage U/XWR/V bits allow or deny VS-mode accesses, and that HS hypervisor loads/stores follow G-stage rules, for coverage.
- **How:** It turns on G-stage only, rewrites the G-stage PTE for each case, enters VS or HS, does load/store or HLV/HSV, checks the results through the signature mechanism, and records coverage.
- **Coverpoint map:** `cp_hgatp_perm_checks, cp_hgatp_pte_g_bit_rw` (`SvH_cg`)

  ```asm
  .set gpa_code, 0x80000000

  csrw vsatp, x0
  SUPERPAGE_G_PTE_SETUP(sv32x4, rvtest_code_begin, (PTE_D | PTE_A | PTE_U | PTE_X | PTE_R | PTE_V), gpa_code, LEVEL1)
  G_PTE_SETUP(sv32x4, rvtest_hlvl0_pg_tbl, (PTE_V), gpa_data, LEVEL1)
  G_PTE_SETUP(sv32x4, test_region, (PTE_D | PTE_A | PTE_U | PTE_X | PTE_W | PTE_R | PTE_V | PTE_G), gpa_data, LEVEL0)
  HGATP_SETUP(sv32x4)
  hfence.vvma

  G_PTE_SETUP(sv32x4, test_region, (PTE_D | PTE_A | PTE_U | PTE_X | PTE_W | PTE_R | PTE_V | PTE_G), gpa_data, LEVEL0)

  sw t0, 0(t1)
  //--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  nop

  nop

  RVTEST_SIGUPD(x2, x5, x4, a3, test1_u1_rwx, test1_u1_rwx_str)
  G_PTE_SETUP(sv32x4, test_region, (PTE_D | PTE_A | PTE_X | PTE_W | PTE_R | PTE_V | PTE_G), gpa_data, LEVEL0)
  hfence.gvma

  nop

  hfence.gvma

  RVTEST_TSBI_GOTO_VSMODE
  sw t0, 0(a5)
  nop
  RVTEST_GOTO_MMODE
  la t1, test_region
  lw s2, 0(t1)

  LI(a6, 0)

  lw a6, 0(a5)
  // ... (106 more lines in twin)
  ```

- **Why those instructions (mapping rules):**
  - `_g_only_setup`; per-case **`G_PTE_SETUP`** (U, XWR, V-only, G-bit).
  - `// Test case N:` → VS lw/sw or HS hlv/hsv → SIGUPD each case.


---

#### T12. `g_perm_VUmode` → `SvH_g_perm_VUmode-00.S`
- **What:** A generated RISC-V Hypervisor test that checks G-stage page permissions from VU-mode.
- **Why:** To verify that VU-mode accesses follow G-stage U/XWR/V bits (U=1 required), for coverage.
- **How:** It turns on G-stage only, rewrites the G-stage PTE for each case, enters VU-mode, does load/store, checks the results through the signature mechanism, and records coverage.
- **Coverpoint map:** `cp_hgatp_perm_checks` (`SvH_cg`)
- **Python entry:** `generate_g_perm_VUmode` in `SvH_perm.py` lines **572-576**.
- **Docstring / intent:** Output ``SvH_g_perm_VUmode-00.S``: G-stage permission checks from VU.
- **Body builder:** `_asm_g_perm` lines **489-566** (one paging twin).
- **Twin / wrap:** helpers used: `build_twins, comment_banner, pte_bits, set_g_data_leaf, twin_data`. Finisher is `build_twins` unless this is misc `_finish_*` or csr `_wrap_sv39_only`.
- **Generated header:** `#define SIGUPD_COUNT 19` in `tests/priv/SvH/SvH_g_perm_VUmode-00.S` (includes writer margin + body dumps; twin uses **max** not sum).
- **Numbered cases in `.S` (first twin, 9):**
  | # | Banner (from generated `.S`) |
  |---|---|
  | 1 | G leaf U=1 XWR=111+G VU sw/lw \| Expected: Successful |
  | 2 | G leaf U=0 XWR=111+G VU lw \| Expected: Expected Fault |
  | 3 | G leaf U=0 XWR=111+G VU sw \| Expected: Expected Fault |
  | 4 | G leaf U=1 X-only \| Expected: Expected Fault |
  | 5 | G leaf U=1 V-only \| Expected: Expected Fault |
  | 6 | Restore U=1 XWR=111+G VU lw \| Expected: Successful |
  | 7 | G leaf U=1 V-only VU sw \| Expected: Expected Fault |
  | 8 | G leaf U=0 V-only VU sw \| Expected: Expected Fault |
  | 9 | G leaf U=0 V-only VU lw \| Expected: Expected Fault |

#### T14. `sum_Upages_VSmode` → `SvH_sum_Upages_VSmode-00.S`
- **What:** A generated RISC-V Hypervisor test that checks VS-mode access to U-mode pages with SUM off, then on.
- **Why:** To verify that `vsstatus.SUM=0` blocks VS access to U pages and `SUM=1` allows it, for coverage.
- **How:** It maps a U=1 page, enters VS-mode with SUM=0 (load/store fault), sets SUM=1, retries, checks the results through the signature mechanism, and records coverage.

  ```asm
  SUPERPAGE_VS_PTE_SETUP(sv32, rvtest_code_begin, (PTE_D | PTE_A | PTE_X | PTE_R | PTE_V), va_code, LEVEL1)
  V_SAVE_AREA_SETUP(va_code, rvtest_code_begin, code, LEVEL1)
  ```

  ```asm
  VS_PTE_SETUP(sv32, PA, test_region, (PTE_D | PTE_A | PTE_X | PTE_W | PTE_R | PTE_V), va_data, LEVEL0)
  VSATP_SETUP(sv32, PA)

  hfence.vvma
  hfence.gvma
  LI(t0, SSTATUS_MXR | SSTATUS_SUM)
  csrc sstatus, t0
  csrc vsstatus, t0
  hfence.vvma
  LI(t0, SSTATUS_MXR | SSTATUS_SUM)
  csrc sstatus, t0
  csrc vsstatus, t0
  hfence.vvma
  VS_PTE_SETUP(sv32, PA, test_region, (PTE_D | PTE_A | PTE_X | PTE_W | PTE_R | PTE_V), va_data, LEVEL0)
  ```

---

#### T16. `vs_perm_VUmode` → `SvH_vs_perm_VUmode-00.S`
- **What:** A generated RISC-V Hypervisor test that checks VS-stage page permissions from VU-mode.
- **Why:** To verify that VU-mode can use U=1 pages and is denied on supervisor pages, for coverage.
- **How:** It maps VS-stage pages, rewrites the VS PTE for each case, enters VU-mode, does the access, checks the results through the signature mechanism, and records coverage.
- **Coverpoint map:** `cp_vsatp_perm_checks` (`SvH_cg`)
- **Python entry:** `generate_vs_perm_VUmode` in `SvH_perm.py` lines **774-778**.
- **Docstring / intent:** Output ``SvH_vs_perm_VUmode-00.S``: VU permission allow/deny on U vs S pages.
- **Body builder:** `_asm_vs_perm` lines **699-768** (one paging twin).
- **Twin / wrap:** helpers used: `build_twins, comment_banner, pte_bits`. Finisher is `build_twins` unless this is misc `_finish_*` or csr `_wrap_sv39_only`.
- **Generated header:** `#define SIGUPD_COUNT 22` in `tests/priv/SvH/SvH_vs_perm_VUmode-00.S` (includes writer margin + body dumps; twin uses **max** not sum).
- **Numbered cases in `.S` (first twin, 12):**
  | # | Banner (from generated `.S`) |
  |---|---|
  | 1 | U=1 XWR=111 VU sw+lw \| Expected: Successful |
  | 2 | U=1 X-only VU lw \| Expected: Expected Fault |
  | 3 | U=1 X-only VU sw \| Expected: Expected Fault |
  | 4 | U=1 R-only VU lw OK, VU sw fault \| Expected: Successful |
  | 5 | U=1 R/W VU sw+lw \| Expected: Successful |
  | 6 | U=1 R/X VU lw OK, VU sw fault \| Expected: Successful |
  | 7 | U=0 XWR=111 VU lw \| Expected: Expected Fault |
  | 8 | U=1 V-only VU lw \| Expected: Expected Fault |
  | 9 | U=0 XWR=111 VU sw \| Expected: Expected Fault |
  | 10 | U=0 V-only VU lw \| Expected: Expected Fault |
  | 11 | U=0 V-only VU sw \| Expected: Expected Fault |
  | 12 | U=1 V-only VU sw \| Expected: Expected Fault |

#### T17. `vsstatus_mxr_sum_VSmode` → `SvH_vsstatus_mxr_sum_VSmode-00.S`
- **What:** A generated RISC-V Hypervisor test that checks `vsstatus` MXR and SUM together from VS-mode.
- **Why:** To verify that MXR and SUM correctly allow or deny VS loads, stores, and jumps on S-pages and U-pages, for coverage.

#### T18. `vsstatus_mxr_sum_VUmode` → `SvH_vsstatus_mxr_sum_VUmode-00.S`
- **What:** A generated RISC-V Hypervisor test that checks `vsstatus` MXR and SUM together from VU-mode.
- **Why:** To verify that VU-mode still cannot use supervisor pages even with SUM=1, while MXR still affects U-pages, for coverage.
- **How:** It sets MXR/SUM, maps S-page and U-page combinations, enters VU-mode for each access, checks the results through the signature mechanism, and records coverage.
- **Coverpoint map:** `cp_vsstatus_mxr_sum` (`SvH_cg`)
- **Python entry:** `generate_vsstatus_mxr_sum_VUmode` in `SvH_perm.py` lines **908-912**.
- **Docstring / intent:** Output ``SvH_vsstatus_mxr_sum_VUmode-00.S``: VU MXR×SUM; SUM does not grant S-pages to VU.
- **Body builder:** `_asm_mxr_sum` lines **779-902** (one paging twin).
- **Twin / wrap:** helpers used: `build_twins, comment_banner, fence_both_stages, pte_bits`. Finisher is `build_twins` unless this is misc `_finish_*` or csr `_wrap_sv39_only`.
- **Generated header:** `#define SIGUPD_COUNT 47` in `tests/priv/SvH/SvH_vsstatus_mxr_sum_VUmode-00.S` (includes writer margin + body dumps; twin uses **max** not sum).
- **Numbered cases in `.S` (first twin, 37):**
  | # | Banner (from generated `.S`) |
  |---|---|
  | 1 | MXR=0 SUM=0 U-page X-only VU lw \| Expected: Expected Fault |
  | 2 | MXR=1 SUM=0 U-page X-only VU lw \| Expected: Successful |

#### T19. `vu_rwx_two_stage_VUmode` → `SvH_vu_rwx_two_stage_VUmode-00.S`
- **What:** A generated RISC-V Hypervisor test that checks VU-mode load/store through two-stage paging with U=1, then U=0.
- **Why:** To verify that VU-mode succeeds on U=1 pages at both stages and faults when the VS-stage U bit is 0, for coverage.
- **How:** It sets up two-stage maps, enters VU-mode, stores/loads with U=1, then retries with VS-stage U=0, checks the results through the signature mechanism, and records coverage.
- **Coverpoint map:** `rwx_umode_pages_umode_rw` (`SvH_cg`)
- **Python entry:** `generate_vu_rwx_two_stage_VUmode` in `SvH_perm.py` lines **1012-1016**.
- **Docstring / intent:** Output ``SvH_vu_rwx_two_stage_VUmode-00.S``: VU on U=1 both stages, then U=0 deny.
- **Body builder:** `_asm_vu_rwx` lines **913-1011** (one paging twin).
- **Twin / wrap:** helpers used: `build_twins, comment_banner, enable_two_stage, mode_names, preload_spa, pte_bits`. Finisher is `build_twins` unless this is misc `_finish_*` or csr `_wrap_sv39_only`.
- **Generated header:** `#define SIGUPD_COUNT 12` in `tests/priv/SvH/SvH_vu_rwx_two_stage_VUmode-00.S` (includes writer margin + body dumps; twin uses **max** not sum).
- **CSR / mode timeline:**
  - Two-stage maps (G + VS); `vsatp` and `hgatp` programmed unless later overridden.
- **Numbered cases in `.S` (first twin, 2):**
  | # | Banner (from generated `.S`) |
  |---|---|
  | 1 | Both stages U=1 XWR=111 VU sw+lw \| Expected: Successful |
  | 2 | VS leaf U=0, G leaf U=1 VU lw \| Expected: Expected Fault |

  ```asm
  VS_PTE_SETUP(sv32, GPA, gpa_code, (PTE_D | PTE_A | PTE_U | PTE_X | PTE_W | PTE_R | PTE_V), va_code, LEVEL1)
  V_SAVE_AREA_SETUP(va_code, rvtest_code_begin, code, LEVEL1)
  VS_PTE_SETUP(sv32, GPA, gpa_rvtest_vlvl0_pg_tbl, (PTE_V), va_data, LEVEL1)
  VS_PTE_SETUP(sv32, GPA, gpa_data, (PTE_D | PTE_A | PTE_U | PTE_X | PTE_W | PTE_R | PTE_V), va_data, LEVEL0)
  VS_PTE_SETUP(sv32, GPA, gpa_deny, (PTE_D | PTE_A | PTE_X | PTE_W | PTE_R | PTE_V), va_deny, LEVEL0)
  VSATP_SETUP(sv32, GPA)
  HGATP_SETUP(sv32x4)
  hfence.vvma
  hfence.gvma
  LI(t0, 0x257a0b01)
  la t1, test_region
  sw t0, 0(t1)

  LI(t0, 0x257A0B01)
  RVTEST_GOTO_LOWER_MODE VUmode
  sw t0, 0(a5)
  nop
  lw a3, 0(a5)
  nop
  RVTEST_GOTO_MMODE
  RVTEST_SIGUPD(x2, x5, x4, a3, test1_allow, test1_allow_str)
  //--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  LI(a4, 0)
  RVTEST_GOTO_LOWER_MODE VUmode
  lw a4, 0(a5)
  nop
  RVTEST_GOTO_MMODE
  RVTEST_SIGUPD(x2, x5, x4, a4, test2_deny, test2_deny_str)
  rvtest_hlvl0_pg_tbl:
  ```

- **Why those instructions (mapping rules):**

#### T24. `hgatp_fault_VSmode` → `SvH_hgatp_fault_VSmode-00.S`
- **What:** A generated RISC-V Hypervisor test that checks G-stage invalid and non-leaf PTEs from VS-mode and HS-mode.
- **Why:** To verify that a bad G-stage PTE causes a guest-page fault on load, store, fetch, HLV, and HSV, for coverage.
- **How:** It turns on G-stage, rewrites the G-stage PTE to invalid or non-leaf, does each access, then restores a valid PTE, checks the results through the signature mechanism, and records coverage.
- **Coverpoint map:** `cp_hgatp_invalid_pte, cp_hgatp_nonleaf_lvl0` (`SvH_cg`)
- **Python entry:** `generate_hgatp_fault_VSmode` in `SvH_fault.py` lines **322-329**.

- **CSR / mode timeline:**
  - Return **M** before SIGUPD (`RVTEST_GOTO_MMODE`).
- **Numbered cases in `.S` (first twin, 8):**
  | # | Banner (from generated `.S`) |
  |---|---|
  | 1 | G leaf V=0, VS Bare lw \| Expected: Expected Fault |
  | 2 | G L0 non-leaf PTE_V only, VS Bare lw \| Expected: Expected Fault |
  | 3 | HS HLV to V=0 GPA \| Expected: Expected Fault |
  | 4 | HS HSV to V=0 GPA \| Expected: Expected Fault |

  | 6 | Restore valid U=1 R/W G leaf, VS Bare lw \| Expected: Expected Fault |

#### T25. `twostage_invalid_VSmode` → `SvH_twostage_invalid_VSmode-00.S`
- **What:** A generated RISC-V Hypervisor test that checks invalid VS-stage or G-stage PTEs during two-stage translation in VS-mode.
- **Why:** To verify that V=0 at either stage faults load, store, and jump independently, for coverage.
- **How:** It sets up two-stage paging, invalidates either the VS-stage or G-stage leaf, does `lw`/`sw`/`jalr`, checks the results through the signature mechanism, and records coverage.
- **Coverpoint map:** `VM_permission_invalid_vs_*, VM_permission_invalid_g_*` (`SvH_cg`)
- **Python entry:** `generate_twostage_invalid_VSmode` in `SvH_fault.py` lines **435-442**.
- **Docstring / intent:** Output ``SvH_twostage_invalid_VSmode-00.S``: invalid VS or G leaf × lw/sw/jalr.
- **Body builder:** `_asm_twostage_invalid` lines **347-434** (one paging twin).
- **Twin / wrap:** helpers used: `build_twins, build_two_stage_maps, comment_banner, fence_both_stages, goto_mmode, goto_vs, load_guest_va, mode_names, preload_spa, pte_bits, set_g_data_leaf, set_va_gpa_symbols, set_vs_data_leaf, twin_data`. Finisher is `build_twins` unless this is misc `_finish_*` or csr `_wrap_sv39_only`.
- **Generated header:** `#define SIGUPD_COUNT 16` in `tests/priv/SvH/SvH_twostage_invalid_VSmode-00.S` (includes writer margin + body dumps; twin uses **max** not sum).
- **CSR / mode timeline:**
  - Two-stage maps (G + VS); `vsatp` and `hgatp` programmed unless later overridden.

#### T26. `vsatp_fault_VSmode` → `SvH_vsatp_fault_VSmode-00.S`
- **What:** A generated RISC-V Hypervisor test that checks VS-stage invalid and non-leaf PTEs from VS-mode and HS-mode.

- **Twin / wrap:** helpers used: `build_twins, comment_banner, goto_mmode, goto_vs, load_guest_va, twin_data`. Finisher is `build_twins` unless this is misc `_finish_*` or csr `_wrap_sv39_only`.
- **Generated header:** `#define SIGUPD_COUNT 18` in `tests/priv/SvH/SvH_vsatp_fault_VSmode-00.S` (includes writer margin + body dumps; twin uses **max** not sum).
- **CSR / mode timeline:**
  - Stimulus privilege: **VS** (`RVTEST_GOTO_LOWER_MODE VSmode`).
  - Return **M** before SIGUPD (`RVTEST_GOTO_MMODE`).

  | 4 | VS sw of L0 non-leaf \| Expected: Expected Fault |

  ```python
  # L502:                 *goto_vs(),  # enter VS
  ```

#### T27. `g_adbit_VSmode` → `SvH_g_adbit_VSmode-00.S`
- **What:** A generated RISC-V Hypervisor test that checks G-stage and VS-stage A/D bits in VS-mode.
- **Why:** To verify that A=D=0 on a G-stage page-table mapping or a VS data page causes a fault, for coverage.
- **How:** It maps pages with A=D=0, enters VS-mode, does load/store/jump, then restores A=D=1, checks the results through the signature mechanism, and records coverage.
- **Coverpoint map:** `cp_hgatp_adbit_behavior` (`SvH_cg`)
- **Python entry:** `generate_g_adbit_VSmode` in `SvH_misc.py` lines **238-242**.
- **Docstring / intent:** Output ``SvH_g_adbit_VSmode-00.S``: G/VS A=D=0 faults, then A=D=1 success.
- **Body builder:** `_asm_g_adbit` lines **195-237** (one paging twin).
- **Twin / wrap:** helpers used: `_finish_twin_test, build_two_stage_maps, fence_both_stages, goto_mmode, goto_vs, mode_names, preload_spa, set_va_gpa_symbols, twin_data`. Finisher is `build_twins` unless this is misc `_finish_*` or csr `_wrap_sv39_only`.
- **Generated header:** `#define SIGUPD_COUNT 16` in `tests/priv/SvH/SvH_g_adbit_VSmode-00.S` (includes writer margin + body dumps; twin uses **max** not sum).
- **CSR / mode timeline:**

#### T34. `mprv_hgatp_Mmode` → `SvH_mprv_hgatp_Mmode-00.S`
- **What:** A generated RISC-V Hypervisor test that checks M-mode MPRV with G-stage translation (`MPP` and `MPV`).
- **Why:** To verify that MPRV+MPP+MPV routes M-mode loads through G-stage when required, and that HLV ignores MPRV, for coverage.

#### T35. `mprv_sum_two_stage_Mmode` → `SvH_mprv_sum_two_stage_Mmode-00.S`
- **What:** A generated RISC-V Hypervisor test that checks M-mode MPRV as VS on a U-page with SUM off, then on.

#### T37. `tvm_hgatp_HSmode` → `SvH_tvm_hgatp_HSmode-00.S`
- **What:** A generated RISC-V Hypervisor test that checks TVM trapping of HS-mode `hgatp` writes.
- **Why:** To verify that `mstatus.TVM=0` allows HS `csrw hgatp` and `TVM=1` traps it, for coverage.
- **How:** It enters HS-mode, writes `hgatp` with TVM=0, then retries with TVM=1, checks the results through the signature mechanism, and records coverage.
- **Coverpoint map:** `cp_hgatp_tvm_effects` (`SvH_cg`)
- **Python entry:** `generate_tvm_hgatp_HSmode` in `SvH_misc.py` lines **595-599**.

- **Body builder:** `_asm_tvm` lines **588-594** (one paging twin).
- **Twin / wrap:** helpers used: `_finish_twin_test, goto_hs, goto_mmode`. Finisher is `build_twins` unless this is misc `_finish_*` or csr `_wrap_sv39_only`.
- **Generated header:** `#define SIGUPD_COUNT 12` in `tests/priv/SvH/SvH_tvm_hgatp_HSmode-00.S` (includes writer margin + body dumps; twin uses **max** not sum).
- **CSR / mode timeline:**
  - Stimulus privilege: **HS**.
  - Return **M** before SIGUPD (`RVTEST_GOTO_MMODE`).
- **Numbered cases in `.S` (first twin, 2):**

  | 1 | TVM=0 HS write hgatp.MODE=HGATP_MODE_SV32X4 \| Expected: Successful |
  | 2 | TVM=1 HS hgatp write illegal \| Expected: Expected Fault |

  ```python
  # L603:     rsw = [("00", "0", 0x257A0301, "s2", "a3"), ("01", "0x100", 0x257A0302, "s3", "a4"), ("10", "0x200", 0x257A0303, "s4", "a6"), ("11", "PTE_SOFT", 0x257A0304, "s5", "a7")]  # RSW name/flag/value/reg tuples

  # L610:         lines.extend(_sigupd_manual(ManualSig(f"test{n}_rsw{name}_sw", sw_reg, f"Mismatch on RSW={name} VS sw SPA"), mismatches))  # SIGUPD + mismatch string

  # L614:         lines.extend(_sigupd_manual(ManualSig(f"test{n}_rsw{name}_lw", lw_reg, f"Mismatch on RSW={name} VS lw"), mismatches))  # SIGUPD + mismatch string
  # L616:     lines.extend(twin_data("sv32", mismatch_strings=mismatches))  # twin .data with mismatch strings
  # L617:     return Body(lines, 8, 8)  # pack lines + SIGUPD/case counts
  ```

#### T39. `vsbe_endian_VSmode` → `SvH_vsbe_endian_VSmode-00.S`
- **What:** A generated RISC-V Hypervisor test that checks `hstatus.VSBE` endianness for VS-mode stores and loads.
- **Why:** To verify that VSBE=0 is little-endian, and to record what the hart does when VSBE=1 is written (it may stay 0), for coverage.
- **How:** It sets VSBE=0, enters VS-mode for store/load, repeats after writing VSBE=1, reads `hstatus` and memory, checks the results through the signature mechanism, and records coverage.
- **Coverpoint map:** `cp_vsatp_endianess` (`SvH_cg`)
- **Python entry:** `generate_vsbe_endian_VSmode` in `SvH_misc.py` lines **735-739**.
- **Docstring / intent:** Output ``SvH_vsbe_endian_VSmode-00.S``: VSBE=0 then VSBE=1 endian check (``vsbe_hstatus``).
- **Body builder:** `_asm_vsbe` lines **677-734** (one paging twin).
- **Twin / wrap:** helpers used: `_finish_twin_test, goto_mmode, goto_vs, set_va_gpa_symbols, twin_data`. Finisher is `build_twins` unless this is misc `_finish_*` or csr `_wrap_sv39_only`.
- **Generated header:** `#define SIGUPD_COUNT 15` in `tests/priv/SvH/SvH_vsbe_endian_VSmode-00.S` (includes writer margin + body dumps; twin uses **max** not sum).
- **CSR / mode timeline:**
  - Stimulus privilege: **VS** (`RVTEST_GOTO_LOWER_MODE VSmode`).
  - Return **M** before SIGUPD (`RVTEST_GOTO_MMODE`).
- **Numbered cases in `.S` (first twin, 5):**
  | # | Banner (from generated `.S`) |
  |---|---|
  | 1 | VSBE=0 VS lw after sw \| Expected: Successful |
  | 2 | VSBE=0 M-mode SPA readback \| Expected: Successful |
  | 3 | VSBE=1 hstatus readback \| Expected: Successful |
  | 4 | VSBE=1 attempt VS lw after sw \| Expected: Successful |
  | 5 | VSBE=1 attempt M-mode SPA byte view \| Expected: Successful |
- **Body Python highlights (live lines, abbreviated):**
  ```python
  # L677: def _asm_vsbe(paging: Mode) -> Body:  # VSBE=0 LE check then VSBE=1 attempt
  # L679:     mismatches: list[str] = []  # mismatch strings for .data
  # L680:     lines = [  # start building asm line list
  # L681:         *set_va_gpa_symbols(paging),  # .set va_* / gpa_*
  # L683:         "  LI(a5, va_data)",  # guest pointer for sw/lw
  # L684:         "  LI(s1, 0xA1B2C3D4)",  # known endian pattern
  # L687:         *_case_header("1", "VSBE=0 VS lw after sw"),  # store/load guest memory
  # L688:         "  LI(t0, HSTATUS_VSBE)",  # VSBE endian control bit
  # L689:         "  csrc hstatus, t0",  # clear VSBE
  # L691:         "  sw x0, 0(t1)",  # clear SPA before guest store
  # L692:         *goto_vs(),  # enter VS-mode
  # L693:         "  sw s1, 0(a5)",  # VS store pattern
  # L695:         *goto_mmode(),  # return to M-mode
  # L696:         "  hfence.vvma",  # asm: HFENCE.VVMA
  # L697:         *goto_vs(),  # enter VS-mode
  # L698:         "  lw a3, 0(a5)",  # asm: load via guest VA
  # L700:         *goto_mmode(),  # return to M-mode
  # L701:         *_sigupd_manual(ManualSig("test1_vsbe0_lw", "a3", "Mismatch on VSBE=0 VS lw"), mismatches),  # load word
  # L704:         "  lw t2, 0(t1)",  # SPA LE view of the same word
  # L705:         *_sigupd_manual(ManualSig("test2_vsbe0_spa", "t2", "Mismatch on VSBE=0 SPA LE readback"), mismatches),  # SIGUPD site record
  # L708:         "  LI(t0, HSTATUS_VSBE)",  # VSBE endian control bit
  # L709:         "  csrs hstatus, t0",  # try to set VSBE
  # L710:         *_sigupd_csr_manual("test3_vsbe1_hstatus", "hstatus", "a4", "Mismatch on hstatus after VSBE set attempt", mismatches),  # VSBE endian
  # L711:         *_case_header("4", "VSBE=1 attempt VS lw after sw"),  # store/load guest memory
  # L713:         "  sw x0, 0(t1)",  # case field string
  # L714:         *goto_vs(),  # enter VS-mode
  # L715:         "  sw s1, 0(a5)",  # case field string
  # L717:         *goto_mmode(),  # return to M-mode
  # L718:         "  hfence.vvma",  # asm: HFENCE.VVMA
  # L719:         *goto_vs(),  # enter VS-mode
  # L720:         "  lw a4, 0(a5)",  # asm: load via guest VA
  # L722:         *goto_mmode(),  # return to M-mode
  # L723:         *_sigupd_manual(ManualSig("test4_vsbe1_lw", "a4", "Mismatch on VSBE=1 attempt VS lw"), mismatches),  # SIGUPD site record
  # L726:         "  lw t3, 0(t1)",  # case field string
  # L727:         *_sigupd_manual(ManualSig("test5_vsbe1_spa", "t3", "Mismatch on VSBE=1 attempt SPA byte view"), mismatches),  # SIGUPD site record
  # L728:         "  LI(t0, HSTATUS_VSBE)",  # VSBE endian control bit
  # L729:         "  csrc hstatus, t0",  # leave VSBE clear for any later code
  # L730:         *twin_data(paging, mismatch_strings=mismatches),  # .data for this paging twin
  # L732:     return Body(lines, 5, 5)  # pack lines + SIGUPD/case counts
  ```

- **Generated assembly spine (first live twin; setup macros + stimulus + SIGUPD):**
  ```asm
  .set va_code,                 0x90000000
  .set gpa_code,                0x12000000
  .set gpa_rvtest_Vroot_pg_tbl, 0x1000a000
  .set gpa_rvtest_vlvl0_pg_tbl, 0x1000b000
  .set va_data,                 0x8001000
  .set gpa_data,                0xc002000
  SUPERPAGE_VS_PTE_SETUP(sv32, rvtest_code_begin, (PTE_D | PTE_A | PTE_X | PTE_R | PTE_V), va_code, LEVEL1)
  V_SAVE_AREA_SETUP(va_code, rvtest_code_begin, code, LEVEL1)
  VS_PTE_SETUP(sv32, PA, rvtest_vlvl0_pg_tbl, (PTE_V), va_data, LEVEL1)
  VS_PTE_SETUP(sv32, PA, test_region, (PTE_D | PTE_A | PTE_X | PTE_W | PTE_R | PTE_V), va_data, LEVEL0)
  VSATP_SETUP(sv32, PA)
  csrw hgatp, x0
  hfence.vvma
  hfence.gvma
  LI(a5, va_data)
  LI(s1, 0xA1B2C3D4)
  // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  //--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // Test case 1: VSBE=0 VS lw after sw | Expected: Successful
  //--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  LI(t0, HSTATUS_VSBE)
  csrc hstatus, t0
  la t1, test_region
  sw x0, 0(t1)
  RVTEST_GOTO_LOWER_MODE VSmode
  sw s1, 0(a5)
  nop
  RVTEST_GOTO_MMODE
  hfence.vvma
  RVTEST_GOTO_LOWER_MODE VSmode
  lw a3, 0(a5)
  nop
  RVTEST_GOTO_MMODE
  RVTEST_SIGUPD(x2, x5, x4, a3, test1_vsbe0_lw, test1_vsbe0_lw_str)
  //--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // Test case 2: VSBE=0 M-mode SPA readback | Expected: Successful
  //--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  la t1, test_region
  lw t2, 0(t1)
  RVTEST_SIGUPD(x2, x5, x4, t2, test2_vsbe0_spa, test2_vsbe0_spa_str)
  //--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // Test case 3: VSBE=1 hstatus readback | Expected: Successful
  //--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  LI(t0, HSTATUS_VSBE)
  csrs hstatus, t0
  RVTEST_SIGUPD_CSR_READ(hstatus, a4, test3_vsbe1_hstatus, test3_vsbe1_hstatus_str)
  //--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // Test case 4: VSBE=1 attempt VS lw after sw | Expected: Successful
  //--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  la t1, test_region
  sw x0, 0(t1)
  RVTEST_GOTO_LOWER_MODE VSmode
  sw s1, 0(a5)
  nop
  RVTEST_GOTO_MMODE
  hfence.vvma
  RVTEST_GOTO_LOWER_MODE VSmode
  lw a4, 0(a5)
  nop
  RVTEST_GOTO_MMODE
  RVTEST_SIGUPD(x2, x5, x4, a4, test4_vsbe1_lw, test4_vsbe1_lw_str)
  //--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // Test case 5: VSBE=1 attempt M-mode SPA byte view | Expected: Successful
  //--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  la t1, test_region
  lw t3, 0(t1)
  RVTEST_SIGUPD(x2, x5, x4, t3, test5_vsbe1_spa, test5_vsbe1_spa_str)
  LI(t0, HSTATUS_VSBE)
  csrc hstatus, t0
  // ... (1 more lines in twin)
  ```
- **Why those instructions (mapping rules):**
  - **`hstatus.VSBE`** clear then set; LE store/load + SPA byte check; VSBE=1 attempt.

## 7. Cross-cutting missing dependencies (all tests)

| Dependency | Where it lives | Why SvH Python cannot substitute |
|------------|----------------|----------------------------------|
| `G_PTE_SETUP` / `VS_PTE_SETUP` / `SUPERPAGE_*` / `V_SAVE_AREA_SETUP` | `tests/env` hypervisor headers | Expand to multi-insn PTE stores |
| `VSATP_SETUP` / `HGATP_SETUP` | same | Program ATP CSRs + root PPNs |
| `RVTEST_GOTO_LOWER_MODE` / `RVTEST_GOTO_MMODE` / T-SBI hops | arch-test env / trap | Privilege change + VA relocate |
| `RVTEST_SIGUPD` / CSR SIGUPD | arch-test env | Signature protocol |
| `hlv.*` / `hsv.*` / `hfence.*` | assembler + ISA | Raw mnemonics emitted by Python |
| Testgen writer | `generators/testgen` | Headers, `SIGUPD_COUNT` margin, file naming |
| GCC/linker | toolchain + linker script | `.S` → ELF |
| Sail + `sail_to_rvvi` | model + `framework/src/act/` | Trace → short Tracefile |
| `SvH_cg` covergroups | `coverpoints/priv/SvH_coverage.svh` | Sampling — needs licensed sim |
| Seeds `_park/svh_seed/` | offline | **Not** imported by generators |

## 8. Commands

```bash
cd riscv-arch-test
# Prefer make's uv path (see act4-riscv-arch-test skill); example:
make testgen EXTENSIONS=SvH EXCLUDE_EXTENSIONS=Sm JOBS=1
# expect 39 files under tests/priv/SvH/

# Functional ELFs / coverage are host-specific; Starter Questa cannot sample covergroups.
```

## 9. Anti-hallucination rules

1. Prefer this walkthrough + live `SvH*.py` over chat memory.  
2. Removed names: `_collect`, `ALL_GENERATORS`, `emit_twin` — use `_SCENARIOS`, `build_twins` (misc: `_finish_twin_test`).  
3. Twin `SIGUPD_COUNT` math is **max**, not **2×**.  
4. Do not hand-edit `tests/priv/SvH/*.S` or `coverpoints/coverage/*`.  
5. Do not claim UCDB coverage without verification-licensed sim + artifacts.

See also: `docs/SvH-Testgen-Plan.md`, `docs/SvH-Implementation-Readme.md`, vault `ACT4-riscv-arch-test`.
